金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 13 日
上週重點訊息
◼ TOPIX 啟動大規模改革：日本交易所集團 10 月 7 日公布 TOPIX 調整結果，符合新制納入標
準的成分股將由 9 月底的 1,636 檔降至 986 檔，另有 35 檔股票新納入。現有成分股中，共
有 683 檔未符合流動性及浮動市值等標準，這些股票將自 2026 年 10 月底起逐步降低權
重，並於 2027 年 10 月重新評估，屆時仍未達標者將於 2028 年 7 月底降至零。此次改革有
助於改善 TOPIX 成分股的流動性及投資價值，並促使企業更加重視市場評價。

◼ 巴西總統選舉波索納洛首輪領先魯拉：巴西最高選舉法院 10 月 5 日公布首輪投票結果，參
議員弗拉維奧・波索納洛取得 47.03%的有效票，現任總統魯拉獲得 45.16%，兩人均未取得
過半票數，將於 10 月 25 日進行第二輪決選。若最終由波索納洛當選將相較有利於巴西股市
表現。
◼ IEA 支持加速釋出既有儲油：國際能源署（IEA）10 月 7 日表示，會員國支持加快釋出 3 月
已承諾提供的儲油，並將優先增加柴油供應。截至目前，會員國已釋出約 3.25 億桶儲油；若
剩餘承諾全數執行，可再向市場提供約 1 億桶供應，有助緩解運輸業與農業面臨的燃料成本
壓力。隨各國持續釋出戰略儲備石油，將有利於在短期內壓抑全球油價進一步上行。
市場展望
◼ 標普 500 獲利預估連續上修，資訊科技為主要推升來源：標普 500 企業獲利預估連續兩季上
修，其中資訊科技產業為主要改善來源，反映 AI、雲端運算及資料中心需求仍然強勁。雖然
美國資料中心仍面臨電力與基礎設施瓶頸，但建置規模持續擴大，AI 投資也逐步延伸至記憶

體、網路設備、資安及企業應用等領域。因此，預期科技類股的獲利動能仍具支撐，具 AI 供
應鏈優勢的台股與日股亦可望延續相關成長動能。
◼ 中天期債券可望兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供給維持高
檔，可能使市場利率維持在較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企業獲利、
現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，較高的美國公債基準利率也使其利息收益維持一定水準。短天
期債券面臨利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較易受到公債供給及利率波動影響，因
此中天期美國公債及投資級債可望在息收與利率風險之間維持較為平衡的表現。

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 09 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 期中選舉「藍色浪潮」，AI 基建政策風險升溫：11 月 3 日美國期中選舉民主黨奪回參眾兩院，
德州新任州長揚言暫停資料中心建設，市場擔憂電力、用水與環境審查趨嚴，AI 資本支出前景
遭重新評價；加上 Fed 官員釋出續升息訊號，資金撤出高評價半導體與 AI 基建族群，美股科
技股大幅修正。短線政策雜音難消，但 AI 需求本質未變，後續觀察新國會實際立法方向。
◼ 科技財報喜憂參半，台股賣壓沉重、政府出手穩市：蘋果營收優於預期但毛利率受記憶體成本

壓抑，AMD 資料中心營收近倍增、高通財報後走弱。美股賣壓延燒，台股連日重挫、新台幣
貶破 33 元，行政院指示金管會機動因應並研議穩定股市措施，官股行庫與投信持續買超。看
法：短線波動仍大，但基本面與政策支撐有助指數逐步築底。
◼ 美國製造業續擴張，台灣通膨降溫：美國 10 月 ISM 製造業指數升至 55.5，連續第十個月擴
張，惟價格分項仍處高檔；台灣 10 月製造業 PMI 連續 13 個月擴張但速度趨緩，CPI 年增率
降至 2.33%。看法：美國景氣與通膨黏性支撐 Fed 偏鷹立場，台灣央行壓力相對減輕；後續
關注 10 月非農就業、11 月 10 日美國 CPI 及 12 月 FOMC 會議。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：科技股中期成長趨勢仍具支撐，
惟短線宜重視個股分化，美國期中選舉帶來的政策不確定性，短期可能使科技股評價承壓，
惟大型雲端業者持續投入 AI 基礎建設，半導體及相關設備需求仍具中期成長動能，科技股整
體基本面仍具支撐，但短期應避免過度集中於高評價、高資本支出及高度依賴外部融資的企

業。優先關注具備技術競爭優勢、穩定營運現金流及較高投資回報能見度的企業。
◼ 中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債仍較具優勢：在美國政策風險升溫及科技股評價調整期
間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源價格的不確定性，現階段不建議延
長存續期間。中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債在兼顧票息收益、利率敏感度與資產配置
穩定性上仍較具優勢。信用債方面，營運現金流充足、槓桿適中及再融資需求可控的發行人
仍較為穩健，對 AI 相關企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合約現金流與債務到期結構。

Document 062: 2026年10月贸易数据点评：外贸增长的价格权重持续上升

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月贸易数据点评：外贸增长的价格权重持续上升.pdf
Report source: 西南证券
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分  
1 
ooo[Table_ReportInfo] 2026 年 11 月 10 日      
证 券研究报告•宏观简评报告     
数据点评 
外贸增长的价格权重持续上升 
——2026 年 10 月贸易数据点评

点评

西 南证券研究院 
[Table_Summary]  摘要：从贸易总体情况看，以美元计，2026 年 1—10 月我国货物贸易进出口
总额同比增长 25.3%，较 1—9 月扩大 1.5 个百分点。10 月单月出口同比增长
35.2%，进口同比增长 41.6%，分别较上月回落 1.1 个百分点、加快 10.6 个百
分点，进口增速创 2021 年 6 月以来新高，贸易顺差收窄至 1052.8 亿美元。展
望后续，去年同期低基数及主要经济体制造业 PMI 仍处扩张区间，有望支撑
11 月外贸增速维持较高水平，但前期集中出货效应减弱、海外需求波动及贸易
政策不确定性或带来扰动。从贸易伙伴看，10 月我国对主要贸易伙伴出口增
速普遍回落，但对韩国出口同比增长 92.4%，较上月加快 24.3 个百分点；自
韩国进口同比增长 110.2%，较上月加快 15.9 个百分点。对韩贸易高增主要受
AI 产业链涨价及加工贸易重复计算效应共同推动，若后续 AI 硬件价格见顶回
落，对韩名义贸易增速或随之放缓。从出口结构看，机电产品、高新技术产品
出口金额同比分别增长 46.1%、66.6%，自动数据处理设备及其零部件、集成
电路出口分别增长 114.8%、137.1%，AI 算力基础设施建设持续提供需求支撑。
不过，集成电路出口数量同比下降 2.5%，金额高增与实际出口数量增长存在
明显背离。汽车及零配件出口金额分别增长 37.6%、32.1%，通用机械设备出
口增长 34.5%，出口动能有所扩散；手机、家电等传统消费电子出口增速边际
放缓，劳动密集型产品出口表现相对偏弱。从进口结构看，机电产品、高新技
术产品进口金额同比涨幅分别扩大至 57.5%、72.1%，集成电路、自动数据处
理设备及其零部件进口分别增长 78.3%、261.0%，但集成电路进口数量同比
持平，反映 AI 产业链进口增长尚未同步体现为实物数量扩张。资源品方面，
原油、成品油进口金额分别增长 41.0%、45.2%，但进口数量仍分别下降 13.3%、
10.2%；铁矿石进口金额同比下降 11.5%，铜及铜材进口金额增长 45.6%，资
源品进口表现分化。汽车进口金额和数量分别下降 38.5%、43.1%，农产品及
粮食、大豆进口也有所收缩。整体来看，10 月外贸增长仍主要由 AI产业链及
部分资源品价格上涨支撑，出口结构向电子产业链集中，进口则呈现高技术产
品偏强、传统资源品及汽车偏弱的分化特征，剔除价格因素后，实际贸易需求
仍有待进一步改善。 
 进口超预期提速，顺差收窄。按美元计，2026 年 1-10 月，我国货物贸易进出
口总额同比增长 25.3%，涨幅相比 1‑ 9 月扩大 1.5 个百分点，持续保持高位。
10 月单月，货物贸易出口同比增长 35.2%，较上月回落 1.1 个百分点，高于
wind 一致预测值（33.25%）；进口同比增长 41.6%，较上月回升 10.6 个百分
点，显著高于 wind 一致预测值（30.36%），创 2021 年 6 月以来最快同比增
速。贸易顺差为 1052.8 亿美元，虽高于去年同期，低于 wind 一致预测值（1195
亿美元），为 5 月以来最小单月顺差。关税与贸易谈判的不确定性催生企业前
置出货，是今年出口高增的重要推手之一；不过随着窗口期临近，前期订单已
逐渐兑现，该支撑的边际贡献逐步回落。同时，近期人民币升值放缓对美元计
价出口的折算贡献有所回落。进口端的高增则主要来自 AI 产业链产品与资源
品涨价，叠加去年同期低基数的驱动。从海外国际景气表现来看，10 月美欧
日制造业景气均有所回落：标普全球美国制造业 PMI 下修至 54.9，新订单增
速降至三月以来最低水平，反映出关税对国际销售的影响。能源、燃料、运输

2
和金属价格上涨叠加关税影响，推动投入成本通胀升至五月以来最高水平；欧
元区制造业 PMI 终值回落至 52.7，产出增速放缓至四个月低点，但整体仍保
持稳健；日本制造业 PMI 终值回落至 53.3，企业产出和新订单增幅均放缓，
且增长速度都降至七个月来最低。贸易政策方面，11 月 9 日国务院关税税则
委员会发布公告，宣布自 11 月 10 日起 2 个月内继续暂停 24%的对美加征关
税、保留 10%税率。此次展期安排有助于稳定外贸企业对美订单与产能预期，
为后续谈判保留政策缓冲期。近期美方同步将 178 项对华 301 关税豁免延期至
2027 年 1 月 9 日，豁免清单以工业中间品、医疗用品、光伏制造设备为主，
有助于降低相关品类对美出口关税成本。往后看，去年四季度基数较低，主要
经济体制造业 PMI 也仍在扩张区间，11 月贸易增速仍有望维持在较高水平，
但动能或边际放缓。出口方面，存储芯片价格上涨仍将提供支撑。进口方面，
芯片需求有望继续支撑年底进口增速，而原油价格环比走弱可能拖累资源品进

口金额。整体来看，AI 硬件仍是影响进出口表现的核心变量，但剔除价格因
素后，真实外需与内需信号或仍相对偏弱。
 单月对韩贸易实现高增，低基数叠加 AI 链涨价共同支撑。以美元计，从出口
总额看，10 月单月我国对主要贸易伙伴出口同比增速普遍边际回落，但是对
韩国出口同比增速边际加快。对美国、东盟出口同比增速分别回落 6.6、5.9
个百分点至 32.9%、46.3%。对韩出口增速加快 24.3 个百分点至 92.4%。从
进口总额看，除欧盟以外，10 月单月我国对主要贸易伙伴的进口同比增速均
加快。对韩国、美国、日本、东盟进口同比增速分别加快 15.9、14.2、11.1、
5.8 个百分点至 110.2%、35.0%、26.5%、42.0%。从韩国前 20 日贸易数据看，
10 月 1-20 日，韩国半导体出口额同比增长 222.5%，占出口总额的 44.7%。
本月我国自韩国进口金额高增主要受 AI 产业链产品价格上涨推动，对韩出口
高增则来自加工贸易的重复计算效应。同一批芯片的中间形态在双边贸易统计

中可能被计入了不止一次，而芯片价格的上涨会同时放大进口额和出口额。若
后续 AI 硬件价格见顶回落，对韩名义出口增速可能随之回落，且实际贸易量
未必出现同等幅度的收缩。此外，10 月中欧就混动汽车贸易安排达成谅解，
拟设置首年 35万辆的进口配额，次年起配额每年上调 6%，措施最早拟于 12
月 1 日生效。该配额机制名义适用于所有来源地混动进口，因此对欧汽车出口
的边际拖累或在年底至明年初逐步显现。
 出口品类集中度提升，AI 产业链为景气主线。从出口金额看，以美元计，10
月单月机电产品、高新技术产品出口金额同比分别增长 46.1%、66.6%，增速
较上月分别加快 2.4、3.0 个百分点，占出口总额的比重分别为 67.42%、33.56%
（两类统计口径存在交叉，部分产品同时归入两类），说明出口高增品类进一
步向电子链条集中。分产品看，AI相关电子产品出口表现突出，自动数据处理
设备及其零部件、集成电路出口金额同比分别增长 114.8%、137.1%，增速较
上月分别加快 21.8、1.1 个百分点。传统消费电子出口增速则有所放缓，手机、

家用电器、音视频设备及其零件出口金额同比分别增长 13.2%、20.9%、12.1%，
增速较上月均边际放缓。汽车包括底盘、汽车零配件出口金额同比分别增长
37.6%、32.1%，而包括箱包、服装、鞋靴等品类的传统劳动密集型产品同比
增速均边际回落，显示传统轻工外需承压。通用机械设备出口金额同比上升
34.5%，增速边际加快 19 个百分点，或反映海外制造业资本开支回暖，全球
中游设备需求边际改善。成品油出口金额同比涨幅收窄 44.8 个百分点至
80.3%。从出口数量看，重点电子产品呈现明显量价分化。集成电路出口数量
同比由上月上升 0.9%转为下降 2.5%，说明本月集成电路出口金额的爆发几乎
完全由单价上涨贡献。手机出口数量同比下降 18.2%，降幅进一步扩大，出口
金额增长主要受高端机型占比提升和产品单价上涨带动，而出口数量降幅扩
大，则反映全球手机终端需求仍显疲弱，也可能与部分低端组装订单持续向海
外转移有关。汽车包括底盘、家用电器出口数量同比分别上升 33.5%、14.1%，

但涨幅均较上月收窄，或由于节前出货、赶关税窗口等因素释放后，增速自然
回落。其中，汽车品类出口量价齐升，但汽车出口数量增速中枢已从二季度的

### Page 3

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分
3
极端水平回落，或反映汽车出口增量逻辑从单纯放量逐步向结构升级、单价抬
升切换。往后看，年内出口结构或延续向电子产业链集中的特征，但需关注价
格上涨与实际出口数量的背离。传统消费电子需求偏弱，手机出口数量或继续
承压；汽车出口则受前期集中出货效应消退影响，增速可能进一步回落。
 涨价驱动 AI 链进口高增，资源品进口读数受价格支撑。从进口金额看，以美
元计，机电产品、高新技术产品进口金额同比涨幅分别扩大至 57.5%、72.1%，
反映进口增长进一步向电子产业链集中。分产品看，AI相关电子产品进口保持
高增长，集成电路进口金额同比涨幅小幅扩大至 78.3%，自动数据处理设备及
其零部件进口金额同比上升 261.0%，涨幅较上月收窄 35.5 个百分点。能源资
源品进口金额有所回升，原油进口金额同比上升 41.0%，涨幅较上月扩大 37.7
个百分点，成品油进口金额同比由上月下降转为上升 45.2%。铁矿砂及其精矿
进口金额同比下降 11.5%，降幅较上月扩大，说明黑色系原料的进口价值量有

所收缩；铜矿砂及其精矿进口金额同比上升 23.1%，涨幅较上月收窄，未锻造
的铜及铜材进口金额同比上升 45.6%，涨幅较上月扩大。10 月 LME 铜期货结
算均价同比上升 35.9%，涨幅较上月收窄约 10 个百分点，但价格同比仍维持
较高正增，或指向铜进口金额高增主要由铜价处于历史高位拉动。汽车包括底
盘进口金额同比下降 38.5%，降幅较上月扩大；农产品、粮食和大豆进口金额
同比均由上月上升转为下降。从进口数量看，电子产品进口呈现价格强、数量
弱的特征。集成电路进口数量同比持平，说明 AI 资本开支周期对价格周期与
订单节奏高度敏感，而实物数量的需求并未同步显著扩张。能源品进口金额增
长主要受到价格推动。原油、成品油进口数量同比分别下降 13.3%、10.2%，
降幅较上月分别收窄 8.2、42.9 个百分点。10 月布伦特原油现货均价为 125.0
美元/桶，同比上升 92.3%，涨幅较上月扩大近 20 个百分点。在原油进口数量
同比仍在下降的背景下，原油进口金额高增主要由价格弹性主导。汽车及农产

品进口则延续数量收缩。汽车包括底盘进口数量同比下降 43.1%，降幅较上月
收窄 6.9 个百分点，本月汽车进口量价两端均明显收缩，而数量端收缩幅度更
大，反映国产车全面替代、进口车市场结构性退潮的长期趋势。粮食和大豆进
口数量同比降幅均有所扩大，本月农产品量价两端均为收缩，反映饲料需求偏
弱，粮食储备节奏偏慢。往后看，集成电路进口金额有望延续较高增长，但进
口数量增长偏弱，价格因素仍是重要驱动。资源品方面，原油价格走弱或削弱
进口金额增速，铜价仍可能提供支撑。整体来看，进口增长仍集中于 AI 相关
硬件，传统资源品及消费品进口需求尚未出现全面改善。
 风险提示：国内政策落地不及预期，海外经济波动超预期，地缘政治风险超预
期。

分析师承诺 
本报告署名分析师具有中国证券业协会授予的证券投资咨询执业资格并注册为证券分析师，报告所采用的数据均
来自合法合规渠道，分析逻辑基于分析师的职业理解，通过合理判断得出结论，独立、客观地出具本报告。分析师承
诺不曾因，不因，也将不会因本报告中的具体推荐意见或观点而直接或间接获取任何形式的补偿。

投资评级说明 
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为公司评级和行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告发布日后 6 个月内
的相对市场表现，即：以报告发布日后 6 个月内公司股价（或行业指数）相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的涨跌幅
作为基准。其中：A 股市场以沪深 300 指数为基准，新三板市场以三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针
对做市转让标的）为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准；美国市场以纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数为基准。 
公司评级 
买入：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 20%以上 
持有：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 10%与 20%之间 
中性：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于-10%与 10%之间 
回避：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于-20%与-10%之间 
卖出：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在-20%以下 
行业评级 
强于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报高于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 5%以上 
跟随大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报介于同期相关证券市场代表性指数-5%与 5%之间 
弱于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报低于同期相关证券市场代表性指数-5%以下

### Page 3

Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
A Closer Look at the Fundamentals
Why we changed our minds – March caution, May upside
surprise
We downgraded Winbond and Nanya Tech in March (Old Memory: Turning Selective) as
elevated DDR4 pricing encouraged supply responses and customer pushback, pointing to
narrower shortages and moderating price hikes. In May, we reversed those calls as lower
expected supply from Micron and Hynix, alongside stronger server and enterprise SSD

demand, widened the projected supply-demand gap (Old Memory: Upside Surprise
Ahead). The distinction matters: March was a selective DDR4 call; today, we see a broader
challenge to old memory’s pricing durability.
What has changed
Pricing power cannot offset weaker demand indefinitely
Our constructive thesis was that niche memory, with limited fixed-price LTA constraints,
could reprice faster and outperform mainstream memory. We now see that flexibility

becoming a two-way risk. Rising uncertainty over cloud spending weakens confidence in
incremental enterprise demand, while much weaker consumer demand challenges
customers’ ability to absorb further hikes.
After the US midterms, our US team now expects ~20% of 2028e US
data center capacity additions to shift to 2029
This include some projects in Texas and Ohio, as governors introduce new data center
legislation (refer to Buy the Drop: AI Investment Opportunities Post the US Midterms by

Stephen Byrd). Stephen has shifted ~5GW of US data center capacity additions from 2028
to 2029  ( Exhibit 11 ) – equivalent to 20% of US and 10% of global 2028 capacity
additions. This implies a 4% Y/Y decline in US data center GW additions  in 2028 before a
38% Y/Y rise in 2029. We now expect global data center additions to increase 9% in 2028
and    20% Y/Y  in 2029.
This could create an air pocket across the tech supply chain in 2028,
potentially reducing pricing power and leading to an inventory build

Our latest PC semis work highlights procurement pull-ins in 1Q–3Q26, followed by a
deeper correction in 4Q, reinforcing the risk that earlier orders overstated underlying
consumption (PC Semis: More Divergent Outlooks).
Supply-demand update –  looking beyond the shortage
Our revised models for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, and DDR4 point to progressively
more balanced markets, particularly into 2028. We now expect 2028 supply-demand
deficits of -3%, -5%, and -1% for SLC NAND, MLC NAND, NOR, with a +1% excess for DDR4.

Near-term tightness need not disappear immediately for stocks to de-rate: a narrowing
shortage can reduce incremental pricing power before earnings peak. We therefore place

less weight on extrapolating recent ASP gains and more on normalized demand and
supply responses and sustainable profitability.

Winbond: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We adjust our EPS estimates +8% for 2026, -9% for 2027, and -22% for 2028:  The
2026 EPS increase reflects 3Q26 actual results. We also factor in a potential peak in
pricing power across NOR, SLC, and DDR4. Compared to the previous estimate, we now
reduce our flash wafer ASP assumptions throughout 2027-2028 and  26% into 4Q28, and
reduce our DRAM ASP per bit estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  23% into 4Q28,

without changing our flash or DRAM shipment estimates. Apart from reduced revenue
estimates, we also lower our gross margin estimates to account for the reduced ASP
estimates. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in
our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these
changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potentially delays in 2028 US cloud capex.

Winbond: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to  NT$120
We continue to apply P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, in line with our
approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in view of the industry's high earnings
volatility. Our price target (base case scenario value) is set at 1.2x 2027e BVPS vs. our
prior target multiple of 2.9x 2027e BVPS, to reflect the potential peak in pricing power.
Our 1.2x target P/B is above our 1.0x P/B valuation anchor –  we still favor the company's

CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for their long-term potential. We see 1.0x P/B as the
downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 58% is almost entirely a function  of reduced target
multiple, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declines only 2% in our new estimate.

Our bull case value is reduced from NT$342 to NT$180, and our bear case value is
reduced from NT$118 to NT$50, based on 1.8x and 0.5x 2027e BVPS, respectively
(previously 3.4x and 1.2x).
We downgrade Winbond to EW
We continue to like the CUBE and S-SiCap businesses for longer-term potential, but we
see near-term pressure from NOR, SLC NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud

capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We believe the stock still has strong
valuation support at 1.0-1.2x P/B, and therefore we believe an EW rating is fair.

GigaDevice: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
We cut our EPS estimates  25% for 2026, 36% for 2027, and 33% for 2028:  The 2026e
EPS change mainly reflects actual 2Q26 and 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across DDR4, NOR Flash, and SLC NAND.
We now reduce our DRAM and others revenue estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 43%
into 4Q28, mainly reflecting changes in our DDR4 ASP estimates. We also reduce our

Flash (including NOR and SLC NAND) ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  25% into
4Q28, without changing our Flash shipment estimates. We did not materially change our
GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of
changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make
these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

GigaDevice: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target  to Rmb259
We continue to derive our price target, which is our base case value, from a residual
income model. We believe this best captures the stock’s long-term value.
Our base case value cut of 65% can be attributed ~33% to earnings estimate revisions, as
has been elaborated, and ~33% from multiple compression, driven by intermediate growth
rate cut and beta increase.  (Note: the overall price target change is calculated from the

fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates are illustrative and should
not be added together or compounded to derive the overall change.).
We cut our intermediate growth rate to 8.0% from 11.3% to reflect stronger changes in
the supply environment compared to the end market demand over the medium to long
term. The potential peak in pricing power for niche memory (DDR4, SLC, MLC, NOR) and
the potential delay of 2028 US cloud capex now suggest slower revenue growth for

GigaDevice from 2026 to 2036. We think a 3.3ppt p.a. growth rate cut reflects the new
supply/demand dynamics with a closer gap.
We also raise our beta to 1.50 from 1.14, leading our cost of equity assumption to rise to
11.8% from 9.4%. This  reflects a cautious valuation scenario, to account for the recent
memory segment volatility, 70%+ correction from the stock price peak in June, and the
fabless model, which does not provide a floor support for valuation similar to the IDM

model. The elevated beta reflects the company's exposure to rapidly changing niche
memory pricing dynamics that could severely affect earnings with a downward bias
compared to our financial forecasts.
Our other key assumptions remain unchanged, including equity risk premium of 6.5%, risk-
free rate of 2% and payout ratio of 40%.
Our bull case value falls to Rmb448 from Rmb1,300, and our bear case value to Rmb112
from Rmb324.
We downgrade GigaDevice to EW

We still see the company as a domestic leader in MCU localization and believe its
customized DRAM business could show a revenue ramp over the next 1-2 years. We also
still like its strategic partnership with CXMT, which provides both capacity support and
process tech improvements. However, we see near-term pressure from the NOR, SLC
NAND, and DDR4 businesses, in all of which industry supply is ramping faster than
demand increase, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to

2029. Fabless design houses do not face depreciation and utilization issues during a down-
cycle, but at the same time they lack a similar valuation anchor as IDM/foundry players
from their book value, and therefore we see potentially continued high volatility ahead.
Our new price target implies a 2027e P/E of 9x, below the historical average of 38x since
2016, owing to changes in pricing power.

PSMC: Earnings Estimate     Revisions
We    adjust our EPS forecasts +10% for 2026 and -11% each for 2027 and 2028:      The
2026e EPS change mainly reflects actual 2Q26 and 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e
EPS changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across DDR3/3 and SLC NAND. We
now reduce our DRAM wafer ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 19% into 4Q28,
without changing our DRAM wafer shipment estimates. We did not materially change our

GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of
changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make
these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and
potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

24
PSMC: Valuation     Methodology
Our price target  is cut to NT$54
We incorporate our revised estimates and maintain our P/B valuation methodology. Our
new target, which is our base case value,  is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down from 2.0x
previously –  below the historical average of 1.5x since 2021, to reflect pricing
uncertainties.
Our base case value/price target cut of 51% is almost entirely a result of target multiple
reduction, rather than change in 2027e BVPS, which declined only 1.5% in our new

estimate. For PSMC, we use 1.0x P/B as the downside valuation anchor –  book value
provides a psychological support level for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during
cyclical downturns. When earnings are temporarily depressed, investors typically place
greater weight on the underlying net asset base.
We decrease our bull case value from NT$177 to NT$86 and our bear case value from NT
$55 to NT$27, implying 1.6x and 0.5x 2027e P/B, respectively.

We downgrade PSMC to EW
We are positive on the company's emerging 3D AI foundry business, and we believe PSMC
is well positioned to benefit from its Micron partnership, including front-end migration to
the one-year  process node and back-end opportunities in advanced DRAM production
(e.g., HBM). The company is also catching up with the DRAM pricing up-cycle.
At the same time, its logic foundry business is set to enjoy continued price hikes into 2027.

However, on its memory foundry side, we see near-term pressure from DDR3/4, in which
industry supply is ramping faster than demand is increasing, especially as global cloud
capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029.
We believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we
believe an EW rating is fair.

Macronix: Earnings  Estimate Revisions
We cut our 2026, 2027, and 2028 EPS forecasts 14%, 20%, and 21%, respectively: The
2026e EPS change mainly reflects actual 3Q26 results. The 2027e and 2028e EPS
changes reflect a potential peak in pricing power across NOR and MLC/SLC NAND. We
now reduce our NAND and /NOR wafer ASP estimates throughout 2027-2028 and 15%
and 8% into 4Q28 respectively, without changing our wafer shipment estimates. We did

not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates are
largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is a
good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating
price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Macronix: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$90 from NT$220
We continue to use a P/B multiple methodology to derive our price target, which is our
base case value, in line with our approach for Greater China memory IDM peers and in
view of the industry's high volatility. Our new target is based on a 2027e P/B of 1.0x, down
from 2.1x previously – below the average of 1.5x since 2017, given the potential peak in
pricing power for NOR and SLC/MLC NAND. For Macronix, we now use 1.0x P/B as the

downside valuation anchor because book value provides a psychological support level for
capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks during cyclical downturns. When earnings are
temporarily depressed, investors typically place greater weight on the underlying net
asset base.
Our base case value/price target cut of 59% reflects both target multiple reduction and a
15% 2027 BVPS estimate reduction (relatively large because of a smaller base).
We cut our bull case value from NT$355 to NT$145, which is 1.6x our 2027e BVPS. We cut

our bear case value from NT$100 to NT$41, which is 0.5x our 2027e BVPS.
We downgrade Macronix to EW
We continue to like its unique position in MLC NAND as the only major supplier still
ramping up capacity. However, we do see near-term pressure from NOR, DDR3/4, and
even SLC/MLC NAND, in all of which  industry supply is ramping faster than demand is
increasing, especially as global cloud capex may face partial delay from 2028 to 2029. We
believe the stock still has strong valuation support at 1.0x P/B, and therefore we believe

an EW rating is fair.

Nanya Tech: Estimate Revisions and Quarterly Financials
Our 2026 estimates are unchanged;  we cut our 2027 and 2028 EPS estimates  7% and
9%,respectively: We factor in a potential peak in DDR3/4 pricing power, which affects
Nanya Tech's top line and margins. We now reduce our Nanya Tech DRAM ASP per bit
estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  6% into 4Q28, without changing our wafer
shipment estimates. We did not materially change our GM assumptions. Our changes in

net  income/EPS estimates are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross
profit estimates. We believe it is a good time to make these changes now as a result of the
narrower shortages, moderating price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Nanya Tech: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$600 to NT$550
Our price target (base case scenario value) reflects a target BVPS multiple of 2.04x for
2027 (was 4.47x) versus ROE of 59% for 2027.  We lower our target multiple to reflect
potential peak in DDR4 pricing power and recent memory segment volatility. For Nanya
Tech, our new base case target P/B multiple of 2.04x P/B is stronger than our downside

valuation anchor of 1.0x P/B for capital-intensive IDM/foundry stocks.  This is because we
expect Nanya to remain relatively resilient compared to other DDR4 players in our
coverage, given stronger process technology and potential DDR5 migration.
Our base case value/price target cut of 8% reflects both target multiple reduction and
BVPS estimate adjustment of 3% arising from earnings estimate revisions.
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$1,270 and NT$330 to NT$1,150 and NT

$300, respectively -- 4.27x and 1.12x 2027e BVPS.
We remain OW
Despite potentially peaking pricing power seen in DDR4, we remain positive on DDR5 in
view of continued strong demand from servers. Nanya is already producing DDR5 on its
second-generation 10-nm process, with DDR5 contributing ~10% of revenue; its next
process enters customer qualification in 1Q27, and EUV implementation is targeted for
2028. This establishes its edge in process technology vs the other DDR4 vendors in our

coverage and a stronger position in DDR5 migration. Valuation is also attractive, in our
view. Therefore, we remain OW on Nanya Tech.

AP Memory: Estimate revisions summary
We  adjust our EPS estimates  +21% for 2026, +2% for 2027, and -12% for 2028: We
factor in 3Q26 preliminary results. We adjust our 2028 forecasts  mainly to account for
supply chain risks. We note that the company's IoTRAM is less affected by  volatility of
typical commodity DRAM pricing. The 2027e and 2028e EPS changes mainly reflect
supply chain uncertainties around S-SiCap. We now reduce our S-SiCap unit shipment

estimates throughout 2027-2028 and  50%+ into 4Q28. We also slightly reduced  our IoT
RAM ASP estimates throughout 2027-28 to reflect peaking DDR3/4 pricing power. In
addition, we reduced our GM assumptions across product lines to reflect potential  peak in
pricing power and supply chain uncertainties. Our changes in net  income/EPS estimates
are largely a function of changes in our revenue and gross profit estimates. We believe it is
a good time to make these changes now as a result of the narrower shortages, moderating

price hikes, and potential delay in 2028 US cloud capex.

Phison: Estimate Revisions
We adjust our 2026, 2027, and 2028 EPS estimates: We factor in 3Q26 results, which
demonstrated continued revenue growth (+27% Q/Q) but also gross margin decline
(59.7% in 3Q, down from 65.3% in 2Q). In 4Q26 and 2027, we expect emerging weakness
from consumer tech and a more moderate pricing environment to result in 4Q revenue
decline, with GM starting to normalize. We think normalized GM is likely to extend into

2028.
We present further discussion of our EPS estimates under Valuation Methodology.

Phison: Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target from NT$2,588 to NT$1,357
Our price target is our base case value, derived from a residual income (RI) model. Our cut
reflects our latest earnings estimates. We also cut our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.0% to 3.0% to be broadly in line with a conservative estimate of long-term
nominal economic growth in developed economies. We believe this is appropriate for

Phison because rising storage demand should support continued growth, while declining
NAND prices per bit, competition, and maturing SSD penetration should limit the
conversion of volume growth into earnings growth, bringing its growth closer to that of
the broader economy over time.
Our other RI parameters are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 9.0%, a medium-
term growth rate of 9.0%, and a dividend payout ratio assumption of 50.0%.
Apart from the earnings estimate revisions and terminal growth rate change, a material

part of the price target change is driven by an error in the previously published residual
income model (August 2026). The error has now been corrected. In the previous RI model,
the 2028e net income was mistakenly calculated as 2027e net income * (1+ intermediate
growth rate), where it should have been linked to the actual 2028e net income forecast.
This overstated the previous price target.
Our earnings estimates have also changed materially  from the previously published

version: they increase 12% for 2026, 60% for 2027, and 37% for  2028. This mainly reflects
higher GM assumptions of 61.2%, 51.9%, and 42.0%, respectively; previously we assumed
60.1%, 43.4%, and 39.5%.
Our base case value/price target is lowered 48%, of which roughly +37% is contributed by
earnings estimate revisions, -6% is contributed by terminal growth rate reduction, and -
83% is contributed by the correction of the prior RI model error. (The overall price target

change is calculated from the fully revised RI model. The individual contribution estimates
are illustrative and should not be added together or compounded to derive the overall
change.)
We cut our bull and bear case values from NT$3,400 and NT$1,250 to NT$1,782 and NT
$655, respectively, implying 8.4x 2027e EPS in the bull case and 3.1x 2027e EPS in the
bear case.
We downgrade Phison to UW
The company hopes its AiDaptive module and service business can pick up and offset the

margin pressure in the conventional module business in the coming quarters, which we
believe is worth monitoring. However, its 3Q GM  of 59% missed the Street estimate of
60.3%; we see this as a potential sign that the previously strong margin profile supported
by low-cost inventory may start to face more downside. Specifically, the AI storage part of
its business (eSSD, and cSSD for AI PC, together 40% of revenue) may face margin
decline. This is what we have been cautious about, and we see the 3Q margin miss as

having provided a good opportunity to downgrade this stock, which we view as

overvalued based on our revised assumptions.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

MPI Corp. (6223.TWO): Richer MEMS mix to drive GM expansion ahead; 
3Q26 beat; TP up to NT$8,600; Buy
12 November 2026 | 12:27AM HKT
Oct revenues on track: Oct revenues came in at NT$2,232mn, +93% YoY and +9% 
MoM (vs. 3Q26 revenues +13% QoQ), accounting for 33% of our 4Q26E revenue 
forecast. Looking into 4Q26, we expect revenue to grow 13% QoQ on continued 
AI-driven demand for both VPC and MEMS probe cards and initial shipments to the 
US CPU customer, and we raise our GM assumption to 62.4% (vs. 60.4% previously) 
to reflect the richer VPC/MEMS mix seen in 3Q26. For 2027, we expect MEMS 
capacity additions, share gains at the US CPU customer and rising pin count per card 
to drive growth, with GM expanding further as MEMS mix rises. 3Q26 beat: Revenue 
was pre-announced. GM of 61.7% came in above GSe/Bloomberg consensus of 
59.8%/59.5%, which we attribute to higher VPC/MEMS shipments for AI-related 
applications. Together with a lower opex ratio of 23.1% (vs. GSe/cons. of 
23.6%/24.1%) on larger revenue scale, operating income grew 26% QoQ, 8.5% / 
11.5% ahead of GSe/cons. EPS of NT$19.18 was 8.1% / 11.0% above GSe/cons. With 
higher GM, we raise our 2026-28E EPS by 5%/3%/1%, and raise our 12m TP to 
NT$8,600 (implied 53x 2027E P/E vs. 90% NI YoY of 2027-28E on avg.). Maintain Buy.

MEMS capacity ramp and new customers to drive growth: Our 4Q26E forecast 
implies average monthly revenue of NT$2,226mn in November-December (flat vs. 
October). On the margin front, we expect product mix to remain favorable for GM, as 
VPC/MEMS capacity additions and new customer ramps continue. Longer term, we 
remain positive on MPI’s growth outlook, driven by 1) MEMS and VPC capacity 
expansion, where we keep our capacity assumption of 2.5mn/8.0mn pins per month 
for VPC/MEMS by end-2027E, which we see as well supported as multiple AI ASIC

and networking programs move into volume; 2) share gains at the US CPU customer, 
where we see potential for it to become MPI’s top revenue contributor in 2027, 
supported by strong CPU demand from agentic AI; 3) content growth, as pin count per 
card keeps climbing, further lift by micro-bump probing at some customers; 4) rising 
PCB insourcing, which should improve both delivery times and margins; and 5) CPO, 
which should open a new growth avenue for its equipment business across Insertion 2 
and 3.

Earnings revisions: We incorporate the 3Q26 results into our model and revise our 
2026/2027/2028E EPS by +4.8%/+3.2%/+1.2%, as we factor in 1) higher GM, reflecting 
the 3Q26 results and a richer VPC/MEMS mix, and 2) higher revenue 
(+0.8%/+1.2%/+1.5% vs. our prior forecasts). Overall, we now model revenue growth of 
63%/106%/47% YoY over 2026-28E (vs. 61%/105%/46% previously), with GM 
expanding to 60.7%/63.1%/63.9% (vs. 59.6%/62.2%/63.7% previously).

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

大量(3167 TT) Buy

CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
投資建議：大量受惠於 AI 帶動 PCB 製造、半導體檢測需求，
獲利大幅成長，CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備：大量在±2 mil 
PCB 背鑽計量領域的地位依然穩固；大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶
動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備需求的趨勢，而非單純受惠某一客戶的拉
貨。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts： 大量 3Q26 營收 36.71 億元，
QoQ+28.40%；稅後純益 8.99 億元，QoQ+65.51%；稅後 EPS 9.85
元。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts，主要來自產品組合優化，包含高階
CCD 機種及 TM3、 TM4。一般機種毛利率約 20~30%，高階機
種毛利率約 50%， TM3、 TM4 毛利率更高，TM4 於 3Q26 出貨
約 5 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：大量先進智能已經於
08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，預計大量
先進智能上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測
(Metrology)、 AOI 智慧檢測(Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。
產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、
CPO 等。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
E：(Environmental)。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持續推
動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低營運
活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持
續推動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低
營運活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技針對廠區內原物料、製程、生產活動
及服務建立環境考量評估機制，評估範圍涵蓋正常、異常及緊急狀況下可能
產生之環境影響，並納入空氣排放、水污染、廢棄物、能源使用、土地污染、
噪音及其他環境衝擊等項目。 
S：(Social)。大量科技重視人才發展與員工照顧，將員工視為公司穩健營運
與永續經營的重要基礎。大量科技秉持以人為本的精神，致力於培育與留任
專業人才，並透過多元招募、薪酬福利、教育訓練、勞資溝通及權益保障等
制度，持續打造友善、平等且具支持性的職場環境。大量科技於人才任用上
遵循相關勞動法令及多元平等原則，重視員工專業能力發展及工作權益保
障，並持續透過教育訓練提升員工技能，強化員工與公司共同成長之基礎。
G：(Governance)。大量科技致力於推動產品朝高規格、高自動化、高智能化
及完全整合化之方向發展，以貼近客戶需求，並與客戶共同開發新產品。此
外，公司持續投入自動化設備之研發，厚植核心技術能力，並持續開發進階
產品，以掌握市場先機。為確保制度有效運作，大量科技於企劃室下設企業
誠信經營小組，由總經理擔任召集人，負責誠信經營政策之推動與執行情形
之監督，並每年向董事會報告執行成果及防範不誠信行為之措施。大量科技
依董事會通過之「誠信經營守則」及相關內部規章，建立對於不誠信行為之
相關防範機制，並透過內部控制、檢舉制度及教育訓練等措施，確保誠信經
營政策之落實。 
公司簡介： 
大量的產主要產品應用於製造、半導體先進製程與先進封裝，以及光電、玻
璃基板與精密材料加工等領域。透過自主研發之控制器、應用軟體、視覺定
位、深度控制、運動控制及量測檢測技術，大量科技依客戶製程需求提供設
備及技術服務。 
PCB 設備：PCB 鑽孔定位、PCB 成型切割、邊緣滾塗、針塗等塗佈製程及
高速型內層銅深度量測機等。 
半導體設備：CMP Pad 即時量測監控設備、3D IC 先進封裝製程晶圓及晶片
量測設備、FOPLP 形貌量測設備、3DIC 先進封裝製程檢測設備、Flip Chip 
製程檢測設備、捲帶包裝晶片本體及外觀缺陷之檢查設備等、封裝製程自動
化設備等。 
大量 PCB 客戶的客戶包含台灣、中國的 PCB 廠；半導體設備客戶包含晶圓
代工廠、OSAT。  
高階背鑽設備的市場，主要同業為德國 Schmoll Maschinen。  
資料來源：大量年報。

4 群益投顧 
PCB 設備包含鑽孔機、成型機和塗膠設備。鑽孔設備也包含量測設備(TM3、
TM4 系列，應用於背鑽檢測)。  
大量有找外包廠商來增加產能： 
考量標準產品與高階產品，大量目前自有的月產能約 400 台，預計到 2027
年每月會達 500 台，大量會再增加外包的產能約 100~150 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：  
大量先進智能已經於 08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，
預計大量先進智能未來上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測(Metrology)、AOI 智慧檢
測 (Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板
級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、 CPO 等。

資料來源：大量法說簡報。 
2027 年 PCB 與半導體設備展望皆正向： 
PCB 設備 2027 年每月都有要交機，交期約 5~6 月；半導體設備 B/B ratio 維
持在 2 以上。 
大量預估 2027 年 CCD 成型機與鑽孔機將維持雙位數成長，TM3 完成階段
性任務後出貨將減少，TM4 出貨量預期會有大幅倍數成長。PCB 在手訂單
有達千台。

產時程預計落在 3Q27。 2027 年設備需求量將倍數成長。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 YoY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 YoY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지
면서 전년 동기 수익성 악화에 크게 영향을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조지아 텔루라이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스포티지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수요가 높은 북미 현지에서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중심의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추정치는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부정적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것으로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상반기 적용한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목표주가를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상대적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모멘텀에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0.pdf

### Page 1

(034020) 
두산에너빌리티 
2026.10.30

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 80,000 원(하향) 
 현재주가 56,700 원(10/29)

 [투자의견] 당사는 두산에너빌리티에 대해 목표주가를 80,000원(2027년 
BPS의 6.2배)으로 27% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 실적은 견조
한 회복을 보이고 있으나, 최근 GE Vernova(GEV) 등 글로벌 AI 및 에너지 
밸류체인 Peer 주가 조정을 반영해 멀티플을 하향 조정했다. 체코 시공 계
약 등 일부 노이즈에 의해 주가가 급락한 만큼 매수 기회라고 판단한다.  
 [3Q26 Review] 매출액 4.4조원(-5.8%qoq, +14.7%yoy), 영업이익 
2,612억원(-16.9%qoq, +90.6%yoy)을 기록해 당사 추정에 부합하는 무
난한 실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티 부문은 매출액 1.8조원, 영업이익 1,211
억원(영업이익률 6.6%, +2.3%p qoq)을 기록하며 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 
고수익 사업 믹스 개선으로 분기 수익성이 재차 개선됐다. 당기순이익은 
149억원(-93.4%qoq)에 그쳤으나, 원달러 환율 급락으로 발생한 환손실 
반영 때문으로 이익 체력과는 무관하다고 판단한다. 3Q26 미국 빅테크 데
이터센터향 가스터빈 첫 출하를 달성했으며, 수주잔고는 에너빌리티 부문 
기준 26.6조원(+70.9%yoy)으로 사상 최대치를 경신했다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 4.8조원, 영업이익 2,640억원으로 유사한 실적 
흐름을 전망한다. 4Q 가스터빈 인도와 체코 두코바니 원전 진행률 반영으로 
에너빌리티 부문 외형 성장이 기대된다. 3Q26 별도 기준 영업이익률이 
8.5%까지 개선된 만큼 에너빌리티 부문의 분기 실적 개선 추세는 유효하다
고 판단한다. 연간 수주 목표 13.3조원 달성을 위한 잔여 수주(3.3조원 +α)
가 예정되어 있으며, 대형원전을 제외하더라도 가이던스 달성은 무난할 것
이라 판단한다. 세부적으로는 SMR 1조원(계약 조건 합의 완료 단계로 연내 
체결 가능), 신재생 7,000억원(해상풍력 기자재·EPC 2건 협의 완료 단계), 
해외 복합 EPC 5,000억원(국내 1건, 해외 2건)이 기대된다. 
 [가스터빈] 3Q26 누적 가스터빈 13기·스팀터빈 11기·LTSA 6건을 달성하
며 연간 10기 이상 수주·수행 역량이 안정적으로 유지되고 있다. 2030년 
슬롯 예약 물량의 본계약 전환은 2027년, 2031년 슬롯은 2028년으로 예
상되며, 수주잔고 증가가 기대된다. 가스터빈 수요 증가를 감안하면 추가 캐
파 증설 결정이 이르면 연내 이루어질 전망이다. 
 [원전] 10월 NuScale 1개 모듈 제작 계약을 체결했으며, PPA 지연 여부와 
무관하게 완결된 계약으로 실적에 반영될 전망이다. X-energy는 연내 기자
재 및 부품의 후속 계약을 추진 중이다. TerraPower 주기기 3종 중 2종 계
약이 완료되어 제작에 착수했으며, Rolls-Royce SMR·i-SMR 등 파트너십 
확대도 지속되고 있다. 이외에 대미투자 관련 기자재 발주는 미국 부지 확정 
및 한국 의회 승인 절차가 남아있어 내년 발주가 기대된다. 기존 웨스팅하우
스 AP1000 Vogtle 계약 대비 업무 범위 확대(원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 
품목) 가능성이 있어 긍정적으로 평가한다.

### Page 2

두산에너빌리티

2_ Eugene Research Center 
설명회 Q&A 
Q1. 4Q26 SMR 수주 목표 약 1 조원의 실현 가능성 
- 고객사와 GTC 등 주요 계약 조건에 대한 합의를 대부분 완료하고 계약 체결 대기 중 
- 연내 계약 가능성이 있어 수주 목표는 의미 있는 수준

Q2. NuScale 모듈 1 기 제작 계약의 PPA 지연 영향 
- 완결된 제작 계약으로 PPA 체결이 지연되더라도 해당 모듈의 제작 및 진행에는 문제없음

Q3. AP1000 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 회계 처리 및 사업 영향 
- DOE·DOC 및 한국 정부 재원을 활용해 EPC 계약·착공 지연과 무관하게 기자재 제작 가능 
- 프로젝트 중단 시 웨스팅하우스의 다른 프로젝트로 기자재 전용 가능성 검토

Q4. 체코 원전 컨소시엄 변경 시 참여 및 사업 계획 
- 현지 지점 운영과 시공인력 상주로 시공 환경 자료 확보 
- 한수원과 초기 인허가·설계에 참여하고, 주기기 공급·설치 역량을 바탕으로 경쟁력 확보 기대

Q5. 체코 원전 EPC 사업의 수익성 확보 가능성 
- 한수원 가이드에 따라 수익성을 고려한 입찰 진행 예정

Q6. 웨스팅하우스 SPV 및 폴란드 AP1000 장주기 기자재 발주 진행 상황 
- DOE·DOC 프로젝트와 폴란드 AP1000 3 기 기자재 조기 발주 협의 중 
- 연내 수주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 수주 추진

Q7. 3Q26 영업이익률 개선 요인 
- 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 사업 믹스 개선이 수익성에 기여 
- 제작 진행률과 마일스톤에 따라 이익 인식

### Page 3

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _3 
Q8. 4Q26 복합화력 수주 목표 5,000 억원의 달성 가능성 
- 국내 1 건, 해외 2 건 협의 중이며 국내 프로젝트의 수주 가능성이 높은 상황 
- 대미 투자 물량은 기존 2031 년까지의 예약계약 납기와 비교해 검토 중

Q9. 3Q26 에너빌리티부문 당기순손실 원인 
- 급격한 환율 하락에 따른 약 800 억원의 환산손실이 주요 원인

Q10. 대미 투자 관련 기자재 발주 시점 
- 미국 부지 확정, 웨스팅하우스 사업제안 및 한국 정부 심사·국회 승인 절차 필요 
- 연말~내년 초 발주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 발주 추진

Q11. 대미 투자 프로젝트의 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 품목까지 공급 범위 확대를 위해 웨스팅하우스와 협상 중

Q12. 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 실적 영향 
- 기자재 출하 시 수금 조건이 충족되며 선발주 자금도 사전 확보 
- 보글·VC 서머 사례에서 기자재 대금을 회수해 프로젝트 지연에 따른 실적 영향은 제한적

Q13. 4Q26 신재생·풍력 수주 목표 약 7,000 억원의 달성 가능성 
- 해상풍력 기자재·EPC 계약 및 유지보수 계약 추진 중 
- 주요 계약 협의가 상당 부분 완료돼 일정 지연이 없다면 연내 목표 달성 전망

Q14. 중형 가스터빈 개발 및 해외 진출 계획 
- 대형 가스터빈은 한국·미국 중심으로 추진하며 중동 고객사와도 협의 중 
- 휴스턴 서비스 허브를 기반으로 미국 데이터센터 시장 대응 
- 중형 가스터빈은 시제품 제작 및 시험 진행 후 2028 년 하반기 출하 목표

### Page 4

두산에너빌리티

4_ Eugene Research Center 
Q15. NuScale 모듈 1 기 선발주 배경 
- 대규모 일괄 발주에 대비한 제작 경험 확보 및 납기 리스크 완화를 위해 사전 제작 추진

Q16. X-energy 연내 후속 계약 대상 품목 
- 증기발생기 전열관 및 주단 소재 등 장주기 품목의 사전 발주 추진

Q17. DOE 프로젝트의 장주기 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 웨스팅하우스 공급망 상황에 따라 추가 품목 협상 중 
- 과거 보글·VC 서머보다 넓은 공급 범위 기대

Q18. 웨스팅하우스 지분 투자와 기자재 계약 시점의 연계 가능성 
- 지분 투자는 내년, 기자재 계약은 연내 또는 내년 초 목표로 시점이 일치할지는 불확실

Q19. X-energy 장주기 기자재의 소재·부품 구분 
- 증기발생기 전열관은 부품, 주단 소재는 소재에 해당 
- 전열관은 공급 병목에 대비해 조기 발주하고 주단 소재는 설계 확정 후 제작 준비

Q20. 복합화력 주기기 수주의 기존·신규 고객 비중 
- 기존 고객과 추가 예약계약을 협의 중이며 신규 글로벌 고객과도 예약계약 협상 진행

Q21. 주요 SMR 사업 외 파트너십 확대 가능성 
- Rolls-Royce 사업 추진 및 GE Hitachi BWRX-300 주단 소재 예약계약 체결 
- i-SMR 은 2035 년 운전을 목표로 2028 년 이전 발주가 필요해 관련 협의 진행

Q22. X-energy 전열관·주단 소재 수주 물량 규모 
- 다우케미컬 초도 사업은 80MW 원자로 4 개 모듈로 구성되며, 해당 4 개 모듈분 부품·소재 공급

### Page 5

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _5 
Q23. 중동 지정학적 불안정에 따른 프로젝트·물류 영향 
- 기존 프로젝트의 운송비 증가분을 발주처·개발사와 협의 중 
- 신규 계약에는 비용 보상 및 공기 지연 관련 조항 반영

Q24. 가스터빈 생산능력 증설 규모 및 시기 
- 현재 생산능력 증설 중이며 2031 년 출하분까지 예약 접수 
- 시장 수요에 따라 추가 증설 검토

Q25. 2030 년 가스터빈 예약 물량의 실제 계약 전환 시점 
- 2030 년 출하 물량은 대부분 내년 실제 계약 여부 결정 전망 
- 2031 년 출하 물량은 2028 년경 계약 여부 결정 예상

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12 개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

2026.10.29   
HD현대중공업  (329180)

3Q26: 조선사는 우직해야 한다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 5조 7,536억원/영업이익 8,992억원 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 매출은 5조 7,536억원/영업이익은 8,992억원으로 
컨센서스(6조 1,295억원/9,671억원) 및 당사 추정치(6조 859억원/9,679억원) 대비 
매출 및 영업이익 각각 -6.1%/-7.0%, -5.5%/-7.1%를 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다.  
조선사업부 매출이 4조 3,988억원/영업이익 6,463억원(OPM 14.7%)이며 이중 상선 
OPM은 14.7%로, 2분기 17.8%에 비해 3.1%P 하락하였으나 1분기 15.9%와 크게 
차이나지 않는 수준을 유지하면서 조업일수 및 환율 하락에도 불구하고 P-MIX및 
빈티지 개선에 따른 효과를 보여주었다. 해양사업부는 매출 3,799억원/영업이익 
1,265억원(OPM 33.3%)로 지난 분기(15.9%)보다도 훨씬 높은 영업이익을 
기록하였다. 엔진기계사업부는 매출 9,511억원/영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)를 
기록하며 역시 견조한 실적을 기록하였다.  
일회성 이익으로 Trion 공사의 C/O 180억원, 일회성 손실로는 셧다운에 따른 
조업감소 영향 500억원 및 환율하락에 따른 이익 감소분 450억원이 있었으며 금액은 
공개되지 않았으나 임단협 잠정타결에 따른 성과급/타결금 지급에 대한 추가 충당금의 
75% 인식이 반영되었다. 알려진 일회성만 제거하더라도 실질 영업이익률은 
17%이상으로 2분기 16.4%보다 높아진 호실적이라고 할 수 있다. 특히 RUYA 공사가 
본 궤도에 오르면서 해양사업부의 이익률이 괄목할 만큼 높아졌으며 지속가능하다는 
회사의 설명이다.

투자의견 BUY 및 조선업종 TOP PICK 유지, 목표주가 530,000원 하향 
HD현대중공업의 목표주가를 530,000원으로 38.3% 하향하지만, 투자의견 BUY와 
조선업종 TOP PICK을 유지한다. 환율 하락 및 28년 하반기 엔진증설 매출확대, 
해양공사 이익률 상승 등 요인을 종합 반영하여 실적 추정치를 미세 반영하여 도출한 
2027F BPS 131,910원에 Target P/B를 기존대비 하향한 4.0배로 적용하였다. 특수선 
기대감 및 선가상승 기대감 하락에 따른 조선업종 전반의 밸류에이션 하락을 반영하여 
Target을 낮췄지만 그럼에도 조선업종 중 가장 높은 멀티플을 부여하며 Top 
Pick의견을 견지한다. 여러 악재에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업은 탄탄하다. 본업인 
상선사업부의 9월말 기준 수주량은 137.9억 달러로 목표치를 이미 초과 달성하였으며 
전사 기준으로도 수주목표 90.5%를 채워넣었다. 수주잔고 또한 상선 421억 
달러/전사 609억 달러로 각각 작년 말 대비 12.6%/13.2% 증가하였다. 해양부문과 
엔진기계부문은 조선사업부보다도 높은 이익률로 든든한 실적의 기반이 되고 있다.  
최근 HD현대그룹은 미국의 EPC강자 Kiewit과 미국 합작법인 설립에 관한 계약을 
맺고 그린필드 기반의 미국 신규 조선소 건설을 추진하고 있다. 빠르면 올해 중으로 
건립과 관련한 세부 사항이 공개될 예정이며 HD현대중공업이 중추적인 역할을 담당할 
것으로 보인다. 일찍부터 브라운필드 방식으로 접근한 동종사와 달리 다소 시간은 
걸렸지만 과감하게 결정된 투자다. 조선소 예상 준공 역시 2028년 이후로 예상되어 
당장 기업가치에 반영하기는 어려우나 엔진 증설과 함께 2028년 후에는 분명한 실적 
상향 요인이 될 것으로 예상한다. 우직하게 본업에 충실하며 신중하게 미래를 준비하는 
조선소, 귀감이다.

Document 254: _美元已死_？不，美元强势归来：DXY突破关键位，空头再遭重创

Source PDF: all report pdf/_美元已死_？不，美元强势归来：DXY突破关键位，空头再遭重创.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

美元指数突破关键技术位，升⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平。美国经济表现、利率优势及外
资流⼊共同⽀撑美元，投机资⾦也持续加码多头。但过去⼀年创纪录的 1.47 万亿美元美国
资产投资，也可能成为美元的潜在⻛险：⼀旦美国资产吸引⼒下降或外汇对冲需求上升，
美元或⾯临回调压⼒。
摘要：
美元多头重新掌握主动权，看空美元的投资者再次落空。美元指数（ DXY ）近期突破关键技术
位，升⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来最⾼⽔平。美国经济数据相对其他主要经济体表现较好，加之利率差异
仍对美元有利，为美元提供了⽀撑。
从技术⾯看，美元指数重新站上 200 周均线，与 2021 年底的⾛势呈现相似形态，且尚未出现明显
超买信号。与此同时，投机资⾦正在回流。据三菱⽇联⾦融集团（ MUFG ）数据，杠杆基⾦美元
多头头⼨已升⾄ 8 ⽉初以来最⾼⽔平，市场看涨情绪有所升温。
美元⾛强的另⼀⾯是欧元持续承压。欧元兑美元已跌⾄ 200 ⽇均线下⽅，初步下⾏⽬标指向
1.10 。不过，美元当前的重要⽀撑因素之⼀ —— 外资对美国资产创纪录的 1.47 万亿美元投资，也

可能成为未来最⼤的⻛险来源。⼀旦美国资产相对表现逆转，资本流⼊放缓或对冲需求上升，
都可能改变美元⾛势。
DXY 突破关键技术位，多头信号全⾯点亮
美元指数近期持续⾛强，最新突破将其推⾄ 2025 年 4 ⽉以来的最⾼⽔平。从技术指标看， DXY 已
重新站上 200 周均线， 200 ⽇均线则持续向上倾斜。相对强弱指数（ RSI ）尚未进⼊超买区域，
上⽅ 105 附近的主要趋势线构成下⼀道重要阻⼒，在此之前，美元仍有⼀定上⾏空间。
更⻓周期来看，⾃ 2011 年以来的美元主要上升趋势线再次提供⽀撑。据 LSEG Workspace 数
据，当前技术形态与 2021 年底⾼度相似。当时美元在类似的技术结构下开启了⼀轮显著升值⾏
情。
技术指标尚未发出明显的反转信号，也为美元多头继续押注提供了依据。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

经济表现差异与利率优势提供⽀撑
美元⾛强并⾮单纯由技术⾯推动，美国与其他主要经济体之间的增⻓表现差异，以及利率差
异，同样是重要⽀撑。
花旗经济意外指数显⽰，美国经济数据整体好于市场预期，但这更多反映的是相对于市场预期
的表现，并不意味着美国经济不存在增⻓压⼒。与此同时， MUFG 数据显⽰，美元⾛势与相对收
益率保持较⾼相关性。只要美国相对于其他经济体的利率优势没有明显收窄，美元就仍将获得
⽀撑。
欧元区的情况则相对不利。欧元区与美国之间的经济意外指数差距⼤幅收窄，反映出欧洲经济
数据相对表现偏弱，欧元兑美元也随之⾛低。
更令欧元承压的是，多项潜在利好均未能转化为汇率⽀撑。能源价格⼤幅下跌、欧洲央⾏释放
偏鹰派政策信号，都未能有效提振欧元，法国财政⻛险则进⼀步加重市场担忧。在相对经济表
现和利率差异均不利的情况下，欧元仍⾯临下⾏压⼒。
外资创纪录流⼊美国资产，为美元提供强⼒托底
除了经济和利率因素，外国资本持续流⼊美国资产，也构成美元的重要⽀撑。
据 MUFG 数据，截⾄今年 8 ⽉的⼀年内，外国投资者购买美国股票和企业债券的规模达到创纪录

的 1.47 万亿美元，接近全球⾦融危机前历史峰值的两倍。
由于外汇对冲⽐例仍处于低位，这些资⾦流⼊转化为直接的美元需求，进⼀步⽀撑美元⾛势。
美国资产的相对吸引⼒不仅影响美股表现，也对美元形成⽀撑。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
投机性持仓同样印证了这⼀趋势。 MUFG 最新持仓数据显⽰，杠杆基⾦的美元多头主要集中在做
多美元兑欧元和加元，其中欧元空头头⼨已升⾄ 2020 年 3 ⽉以来最极端⽔平之⼀。
这意味着，美元⾛强已不只是基本⾯因素推动的结果，投机资⾦也在进⼀步强化这⼀趋势。
1.47 万亿美元资本流⼊：最⼤⽀撑或成最⼤⻛险
不过，当前⽀撑美元的最强劲⼒量，未来也可能成为其最⼤的⻛险来源。
MUFG 警告，如果美国股市相对其他市场的表现持续逆转，外国投资者对美国资产的需求可能随
之降温，进⽽削弱美元的资本流⼊⽀撑。
值得注意的是，美元⾛弱并不⼀定需要外国投资者⼤规模抛售美国资产。即使他们继续持有美
股和企业债券，只要开始提⾼美元对冲⽐例，也可能显著改变外汇市场的供需关系，削弱此前

由资本流⼊带来的美元需求。
因此，下⼀轮美元趋势逆转的导⽕索，可能⾸先出现在美国股市，⽽⾮外汇市场本⾝。 MUFG 认
为，美国股市相对表现和外国投资者的持仓变化，将是判断美元强势能否持续的关键指标。
短期来看，技术⾯、利差和资⾦流向仍共同⽀持美元多头。但如果美国资产的相对吸引⼒开始
减弱，当前⽀撑美元的庞⼤资本流⼊，也可能逐渐转化为美元上涨周期的隐患。

Document 291: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research – BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

### Page 3

3
Advanced Economies: Resilience to shock after shock (forecasts as of September 30)

Document 307: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53.2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities in the coming 6-12 months, 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15-20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid-to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid-cycle bull market. Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

Allocation to global equities decrease to 52.5% from 
53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio 
Foundation tactical asset allocation

Source: Standard Chartered 
Allocations in the chart are rounded to the nearest whole number

Our forecasts imply 9% upside in the S&P 500 over the 
next 12m, as of 29 October 2026

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex-UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low-teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities. We expect the 
segment to generate 10-15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years, with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

US and Asia ex-Japan would continue to lead in 
earnings growth in 2027

earnings growth in 2027, led by technology and 
healthcare sectors

### Page 3

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High-Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks, MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advances in oncology and innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks 
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. Healthcare and 
consumer staples accounts for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management in a mid-
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and potentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge 
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two, and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets, private equity provides a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, infrastructure offers differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where contracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3-7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell-off caused by AI-
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi-sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010-2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress. 
— Steve Brice, Global Chief Investment Officer (CIO)  
— Manpreet Gill, CIO, EMEA 
— Raymond Cheng, CFA, CPA, CIO, North Asia 
— Sundeep Gantori, CFA, CAIA, CIO, Equities 
— Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

M31 (6643 TT)

維持評等 
增加持股(Overweight) 
隨權利金營收比重提升，未來營運狀況更趨穩定 
⚫ 3Q26營收增幅低於預期 
⚫ 3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數 
⚫ 提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均 
3Q26營收增幅低於預期：M31 3Q26營收為 4.95億元(QoQ+17.4%，
YoY+39.6%)，以美金計價營收為 1,546 萬美元 (QoQ+16.2%，
YoY+31.0%)，營收成長受惠於客戶開案動能強勁、權利金穩健成長，
以及北美高價值專案營收認列。營業費用為 4.8 億元(QoQ+11.2%，
YoY+21.1%)，因調整部分獎金發放時點而使本季營業費用較高。營業
利益為 730萬元，相較 2Q26由虧轉盈。EPS為 0.35元。 
3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數：3Q26授權金收入為 4.06億
元(QoQ+17%，YoY+47%)，約佔營收 82%，創歷史單季第三高。Fabless
授權金收入以美元計算 QoQ+3%，YoY+127%，主要由北美客戶先進
製程專案及晶圓代工平台合作案帶動，其中包含公司首個 2奈米以下
的 GAA架構專案，Fabless客戶持續提升對 16奈米以下先進製程技術
的採用比例，先進製程專案已成為授權業務成長的主要驅動力。3Q26
權利金收入為 0.89億元(QoQ+19%，YoY+14%)，約佔營收 18%，創
1Q23以來新高，為近 15季最佳表現，Foundry權利金收入持續成長，
反映既有平台專案逐步放量並持續貢獻營收，未來 A14、2奈米及 CSP
等新案機會可望進一步延續公司於先進製程領域的成長動能。 
提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均：公司將於 4Q26持續深
化與北美 IDM客戶客製化記憶體平台、美系晶圓廠 12奈米平台的合
作，完成與大型晶圓廠 28奈米 Foundation IP合約簽署，2奈米以下
與多項先進製程專案持續開展，將帶動授權金維持高檔。公司目標
2026年 EPS轉正，且全年營收較 2025年成長。2027年授權金目標成
長約 10%，權利金挑戰 30%以上成長，隨著更多客戶專案逐步進入大
量生產階段，預期先進製程權利金收入將進入加速成長期，16奈米以
下先進製程權利金比重將提升至 50%。預期 2027年整體營收分布更
為平穩，目標上下半年分別為 45:55。 
我們基於 2027年預估 EPS 6.35元*60倍 PER，調整目標價至 381元，
維持增加持股評等。風險因子:專案進程與製程平台導入進度。

Document 416: What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
WHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM
THE MEASURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO
TURN THE SCREW?
Marianne Mueller and Pascal Devaux
issue 19
21 October 2026
The 2022 energy crisis clearly highlighted the issue of electricity prices in Europe — and, more broadly, the issue of energy prices. As
a result, European manufacturers’ competitiveness has been severely undermined when compared with the United States and China.

By reigniting tensions over electricity prices, the conflict that began in the Middle East in late February 2026 further underscores the
continent’s vulnerability to fossil fuel imports, despite the expansion of renewable energy. Faced with an environment of potential sharp
and sudden price rises, European governments do not have the same room for manoeuvre.
While the additional costs borne by European manufacturers are mainly linked to the share of gas in national electricity mixes, we will

first illustrate that, in Germany, grid operation and maintenance costs, as well as taxation, are also contributing significantly to these
costs.
We will then explore the solutions proposed by the German government under the new European measures regulating state aid to
electricity-intensive industries.
Finally, our estimates will show that, while these measures make Germany more competitive against some European countries, they
do not place it on an equal competitive footing with the United States or China. Nevertheless, Germany has made significant progress

in decarbonising and making its electricity mix more flexible from a European perspective. This should help to ensure that electricity costs
are reduced in the medium term.

### Page 2

2
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoInsight 19 / 21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
worldWHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM THE MEA- SURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO TURN THE SCREW?
GAS: A KEY FACTOR IN THE ADDITIONAL ELECTRICITY COSTS
FACED BY EUROPEAN INDUSTRY
European manufacturers are still paying significantly higher elec -
tricity costs than their American and Chinese competitors. In 2019,

there was a 30% difference between the prices charged to European
electricity-intensive industries (corresponding to the lower end of the
industrial price range) and the average US industrial price. In 2025,
it stood at nearly 100% according to the IEA (2026) 1, after peaking at
an average of 148% between 2022 and 2023. There is currently a 50%
difference with their Chinese competitors2.
The disruption to Russian gas supplies since 2022 is behind the rising

gas prices, particularly as the EU has had to turn to liquefied natural
gas3 (LNG), which is more expensive than gas transported via pipe -
line and, above all, exposes Europe to global gas market conditions.
The growth of LNG has, in fact, intensified competition between the
major consumption regions, primarily Europe and Asia. European and
Asian prices now tend to move more in tandem (Chart 1). However, in
China, the prevalence of coal — a widely available local resource —

and a more regulated electricity price help to keep industrial prices
lower. At the same time, the United States enjoys plentiful domestic
gas, which is produced at a low cost, driven by the sharp rise in shale
oil and gas production over the past decade. By contrast, since 2022,
Europe has been more exposed to the cost of imported gas (90% of its
total consumption, according to Eurostat), while the United States and
China have enjoyed plentiful domestic resources.

Fluctuations and the level of gas prices are passed on to Eu -
ropean electricity prices via the ‘merit order’ marginal pricing
mechanism, with the wholesale price determined by the margi -
nal cost of the last (and most expensive) electricity source called
upon to meet demand.
As a result, gas plays a decisive role, with gas-fired power sta -
tions setting the price in around 40% of cases in Europe4, with si-
gnificant variations depending on national electricity mixes. It is

close to this average in Germany, below it in Spain (26%) and
France (17%), and above it in Italy (73%) 5 in 2025 6 7. Therefore,
electricity prices are still sensitive to fluctuations in gas prices,
even when electricity is not generated from gas (Chart 2). This
pass-through is particularly pronounced when demand rises
and requires the use of more expensive sources, or when supply
constraints arise (for example, during extreme heatwaves that
reduce wind-power generation or disrupt the cooling of nuclear

power plants).
1 Prices – Electricity 2026 – Analysis – IEA
2 The difference may be even larger for other manufacturers who do not receive the same support as electricity-intensive sectors.
3 Imports of Russian gas (via pipeline and LNG) accounted for around 45% of total European imports in 2021, compared with 14% in Q2 2026 (Bruegel, natural gas imports).
4 ACER, 2026, Key developments in EU electricity and gas markets, 2026 Monitoring report.
5 In Italy, in response to sharply rising gas prices in 2026, the government is currently negotiating a potential option of capping the prices of gas supplied to power stations in

order to limit the rise in the wholesale price of electricity. However, this can only be a temporary general mechanism, with the potentially major cost being borne by public funds
and/or households.
6 Eurelectric, 2026, Power Barometer.
7 EMBER, 2026, Latest energy shock reminds Europe of its risky reliance on gas.
8 Energy shock: 2026 vs. 2022 dashboard – 2nd October 2026 – Economic Studies – BNP Paribas
However, for the time being, the 2026 energy crisis is having a far

smaller effect on electricity prices than in 2022. On the one hand, the
2026 peak reached by the European TTF benchmark is still well below
that recorded in 20228.
In addition, there has been significant progress in decarbonising Eu -
ropean electricity mixes since 2022, which has helped to mitigate, on
average, the pass-through of gas prices to electricity prices.

SOME EUROPEAN COUNTRIES HAVE MANAGED TO STAY
COMPETITIVE, BUT GERMANY IS STILL THE EXCEPTION
Electricity prices partly vary due to countries’ electricity mixes
(Chart 3). Electricity-intensive manufacturers in countries where the
share of fossil energy is high face higher costs (Poland, Italy and Ire -
land) (Chart 4). Conversely, countries with a less carbon-intensive mix

(particularly France and Spain in recent times) are less directly ex -
posed to these fluctuations, with costs closer to the United States and
China (Chart 5).
The situation in Germany is paradoxical, as, despite progressively
greening its electricity mix and wholesale electricity prices (averaging
90 EUR/MWh over the 2023–2025 period) and average electricity costs
(across all industrial sectors) in line with the European average, the most
energy-intensive industries face higher costs than the European average

(and even more so compared with international competitors). This is
mainly due to grid costs and a tax burden above EU standards (Chart 6).
9 Eurostat data are used to compare prices with the rest of the EU. However, given that electricity prices are not uniform for businesses at a European level, there may be differences
between Eurostat and national sources. Jäger P., 2025, “Cautionary tale or model for Europe? How member states should react to Germany’s new electricity subsidy”, Hertie School,

Jacques Delors Centre.
10 P. Devaux, 2026, “European Union: low carbon transition and energy sovereignty, a path fraught with obstacles”
In the first half of 2026, for the most electricity-intensive industries,
the difference compared with the European average was 9%, based on
the prices excluding taxes and levies provided by Eurostat. This gap is
mainly due to the cost of accessing the grid. If all taxes and levies are
included, the difference rises to 21% (Chart 7)9.

EUROPE’S APPROACH OF ADOPTING BOTH STRUCTURAL
PROGRAMMES AND TARGETED SUPPORT MEASURES
European programmes to support the low-carbon transition pursue
objectives relating to climate action (reducing greenhouse gas emis -
sions), sovereignty10 (increasing energy self-sufficiency) and competi -
tiveness (reducing the volatility and costs associated with fossil fuels).
The RePowerEU programme, which was launched in 2022 in response
to the energy crisis, primarily aims to reduce imports of Russian gas

and, more generally, to accelerate the low-carbon transition. Although
Europe has reduced its dependence on imports of Russian fossil fuels,
there has still not been enough progress around the energy transition
and cross-border connectivity. This is due to governance constraints
